<!-- ty-source-background:start key=symbolic-source-anchor reason=markdown-structure -->
<a id="symbolic-denotation-efficiency"></a>
<!-- ty-source-background:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=symbolic-denotation-efficiency-result kind=outcome_result -->
The Harness accepts a versioned symbolic UI design-fact representation only when it preserves the complete V1 extensional denotation and current proof semantics while avoiding work proportional to the theoretical ground Cartesian universe, retaining V1 compatibility, one Authority and one Final Gate.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=original-attachment-provenance kind=requirement -->
The original proposal is the attachment C:/Users/777/.codex/attachments/bf58ac35-8be1-46c2-9589-c4ae10dd4785/pasted-text.txt with SHA-256 45f8e30e9dae917739433456e741f6cb4c4ce64ae513023274a83195234e5b93; all retained delivery meaning is atomized in this Source.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=audited-source-supersession kind=requirement -->
This audited Source supersedes conflicting mechanisms, scope, theorem claims and rollout choices in the original attachment; implementation must follow this Source rather than reproduce the attachment verbatim.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=complete-material-input-index kind=technical_obligation -->
The immutable attachment identity, user-supplied amendments, adopted audit blockers, controlling Context, canonical repository specifications and preservation documents must all be indexed with exact provenance and no material fragment hidden as supporting-only.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=complete-extensional-universe kind=requirement -->
V1 and V2 equivalence is defined over the complete extensional Universe, not over sampled rows, currently materialized rows or aggregate labels.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=subject-relation-universe-axis kind=requirement -->
Every applicable subject and every typed multi-subject relation is a first-class axis of the extensional Universe with stable identity and endpoint roles.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=target-universe-axis kind=requirement -->
Every selected target is a first-class axis of the extensional Universe and target identity may not be inferred from a representative platform or default route.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=reachable-condition-variation-universe-axis kind=requirement -->
Every reachable target condition and subject-local variation combination is present in the extensional Universe or is removed by an exact basis-backed unreachable disposition.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=applicable-atomic-property-universe-axis kind=requirement -->
Every applicable atomic property is a separate extensional semantic point; broad dimensions, aggregate property labels and implementation-defined property discovery cannot replace it.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=population-quantifier-universe-axis kind=requirement -->
Every static or dynamic population and its exact quantifier semantics are part of the extensional Universe, including universe identity, enumeration or partition rule and exclusions.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=pointwise-disposition-equivalence kind=requirement -->
At every extensional Universe point, V1 and V2 must yield the same disposition, including specified, not-applicable, excluded, decision-required, unavailable and blocking effects.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=pointwise-expected-semantics-equivalence kind=requirement -->
At every applicable extensional Universe point, V1 and V2 must yield semantically equal expected value, type, locator authority, provenance, sensitivity and quantifier meaning.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=pointwise-proof-denotation-equivalence kind=requirement -->
At every applicable extensional Universe point, V1 and V2 must yield the same complete proof-obligation denotation: required methods, proof surfaces, comparator and parameters, tolerance or mask, Oracle, environment, protected-value policy and completion effect.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=v1-ground-row-identity-not-required kind=requirement -->
Equivalence does not require V2 to preserve the physical identity, ordering or one-row-per-cell layout of V1 ground Fact Cells; only complete extensional denotation and externally frozen V1 compatibility are preserved.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=fact-identity-proof-obligation-separation kind=technical_obligation -->
A semantic Fact identity denotes one expected predicate independently of proof method; Fact identity, semantic proof obligation identity and representation-certificate identity remain separate typed namespaces.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=required-method-denotation-preservation kind=technical_obligation -->
V2 must preserve every property-required verification method and the furthest independently failing observation boundary; one convenient method or certificate cannot discharge a different required method.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=comparison-oracle-environment-preservation kind=technical_obligation -->
Existing exact expected locators and digests, comparator parameters, authoritative tolerance or mask, Oracle identity, deterministic environment identity and current verdict semantics remain frozen through V2 projection.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=protected-observation-preservation kind=technical_obligation -->
Protected observations may remain behind immutable artifact locators and digests, but V2 may not weaken sensitivity classification, redaction policy, attribution or falsifiability.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=exact-target-layout-pixel-preservation kind=requirement -->
Every exact target retains full-target layout-geometry and visual-pixel Facts for every applicable condition and retains their distinct proof methods under V2.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=canonical-resource-value-authority kind=technical_obligation -->
Immutable canonical design resources remain the sole owner of exact design values; the handoff, Contract, DAG and certificates store identities, locators and bindings rather than copied expected values.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=versioned-ui-symbolic-v2 kind=technical_obligation -->
Introduce a distinct versioned UI symbolic manifest and handoff V2 while leaving the existing V1 schema, parser and semantics intact.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=ui-v2-explicit-opt-in kind=requirement -->
UI V2 is explicit opt-in for each selected design target; absence of the V2 discriminator continues to select V1.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=mixed-v1-v2-targets-one-contract kind=requirement -->
One Long-Task Contract may contain V1 and V2 selected design targets simultaneously, with each target validated and evidenced under its own declared representation.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=restricted-symbolic-predicate-dsl kind=technical_obligation -->
The production symbolic engine accepts only a package-owned, versioned, strict predicate DSL with enum equality or membership, bounded numeric ranges, all, any and not; unknown operators and arbitrary executable predicates fail closed.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=finite-enum-and-bounded-numeric-domains kind=technical_obligation -->
Every symbolic axis has an explicitly finite enum domain or a bounded numeric domain with exact boundary semantics; open-ended or implementation-discovered domains are unavailable.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=canonical-shared-decision-dag kind=technical_obligation -->
Production normalization uses one manifest-level package-owned canonical ordered multi-valued decision DAG compilation session with shared axis partitions, predicate and Boolean memoization, hash-consed nodes, shared subtrees and tuple, profile, Rule, disposition and Census indexes; one logical point lookup scans only indexed local candidates.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=unreachable-branch-elimination kind=technical_obligation -->
Canonicalization removes unreachable branches using declared domain theory without enumerating the full Cartesian assignment space.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=no-production-ground-materialization kind=technical_obligation -->
Production parsing, validation, canonicalization, Contract projection and evidence comparison must not materialize the theoretical ground Cartesian universe, including as an intermediate later deduplicated form.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=mutually-exclusive-effective-regions kind=requirement -->
Canonical effective regions for a subject, target, property and population semantic point are mutually exclusive; overlapping specified or disposition regions fail instead of using precedence.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=exact-region-coverage-no-gaps kind=requirement -->
Canonical effective regions exactly partition every reachable applicable or explicitly disposed domain; any gap, duplicate coverage or unreachable claimed region fails with attributable identities.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=constant-located-expected-rule-values kind=requirement -->
The first V2 Rule representation permits only one constant located expected value per effective Rule region; the value locator and digest are canonical-resource authority.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=no-rule-precedence-or-value-function kind=technical_obligation -->
The first V2 rejects ordered override precedence, computed expected-value functions, arbitrary code and late implementation-owned value resolution because they weaken canonical mutual exclusion and equivalence proof.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=deterministic-canonical-identity kind=technical_obligation -->
Equivalent symbolic inputs normalize to the same canonical DAG serialization and digest independent of declaration ordering, redundant predicates or chunk boundaries; adding an unrelated numeric partition cut cannot change an existing Rule's canonical DAG, digest or canonical byte count.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=symbolic-complexity-fuse kind=technical_obligation -->
Fail-closed complexity fuses bound predicate depth, input predicate nodes, canonical DAG nodes, partition edges and canonical bytes; exhaustion reports unavailable and never samples or truncates.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=ground-cardinality-diagnostic-only kind=technical_obligation -->
Theoretical ground cardinality is computed with overflow-safe integer arithmetic for diagnostics and admission comparisons only; it is never used to allocate or iterate a ground array in production.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=package-policy-property-catalog kind=requirement -->
Property applicability starts from the complete package-owned standard property catalog plus validated custom properties; package-owned capability and property profiles may remove physical subject-by-property N/A rows only while every logical subject-property point resolves to one unique disposition, and the Inspector cannot silently narrow that candidate catalog.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=inspector-census-applicability kind=requirement -->
Every property region reported applicable and the exact custom-property set must be supported by the frozen Inspector Census, capability declaration, inspected input closure and exact subject or relation identity; every instance exception carries exact Census, Source and basis authority plus a non-empty rationale and must change its profile-derived disposition.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=basis-backed-property-remainder kind=requirement -->
For every logical package-catalog or validated custom property point not applicable in a reachable region, including points with no physical matrix row, the complete remainder is covered by an exact basis-backed not-applicable, excluded, decision-required or unavailable disposition.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=unknown-applicability-blocks kind=requirement -->
Unknown Inspector capability, unreadable input, unresolved custom property, incomplete Census or uncovered applicability remainder blocks ready rather than defaulting to not-applicable.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=relation-population-first-class-model kind=technical_obligation -->
Symbolic Fact Rules model local subjects, typed relations, static populations and dynamic populations directly; relation endpoints or population members cannot be hidden in labels.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=population-quantifier-denotation kind=technical_obligation -->
Each Rule and semantic obligation preserves exact one, all, any, none, exactly, at-least, at-most or range quantifier meaning and its population reference.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=v2-rule-method-region-projection kind=technical_obligation -->
For an opted-in V2 target, Long-Task compilation projects each semantic Fact Rule to every required method and only the necessary canonical method region, without reconstructing V1 ground rows.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=stable-symbolic-fact-rule-identity kind=technical_obligation -->
V2 Fact Rule identities are stable under canonical-equivalent predicate rewrites while semantic proof obligations and certificate obligations retain distinct identities.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=per-method-region-obligations kind=technical_obligation -->
Every property-required method receives an independently attributable semantic obligation over its exact canonical region, comparator, Oracle, environment and observation boundary.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=typed-v2-rule-results kind=technical_obligation -->
Current-candidate V2 evidence contains typed per-Rule and per-method-region results with attributable actual observation, comparison, verdict, Oracle, environment and protected-value policy.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=typed-certificate-results kind=technical_obligation -->
Current-candidate V2 evidence contains typed separately evaluated non-interference certificate results; a certificate declaration without a fresh recomputed result proves nothing.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=current-snapshot-evidence-only kind=requirement -->
All V1 and V2 semantic and certificate results are produced from the same current final candidate after the last controlling-input change; Progress, receipts and historical passing results are non-authoritative.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=mixed-target-one-final-gate kind=technical_obligation -->
The existing Long-Task Final Gate evaluates mixed V1 and V2 target expectations together and remains the sole current-snapshot acceptance carrier.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=set-valued-noninterference-certificates kind=requirement -->
One set-valued non-interference certificate may cover multiple Rule identities and omitted axes when package preflight validates that exact set against trusted Source-side and production-side proofs; physical Rule-by-axis dependency edges are not certificate rows.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=certificate-omitted-axis-coverage kind=requirement -->
Non-interference accounting reports the exact set and count of omitted axes covered by each certificate and rejects duplicate Rule or axis references, unknown axes, reachable-but-uncovered omissions and semantically relevant omissions.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=certificate-dependency-edge-coverage kind=requirement -->
The physical certificate dependency-edge set and count are exactly empty; dependency causation belongs to memoized frozen Source-side and production-side proof DAGs whose complete input closure, cycles, dangling or unreachable nodes, Rule-root axes and bounded node, edge and depth capacity are validated before a causally relevant omitted axis is rejected.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=noninterference-obligation-count-metric kind=requirement -->
Admission and regression evidence reports semantic obligation count and certificate obligation count separately.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=noninterference-dag-node-count-metric kind=requirement -->
Admission and regression evidence reports canonical DAG node and partition-edge counts for each fixture.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=noninterference-byte-count-metric kind=requirement -->
Admission and regression evidence reports canonical serialized bytes and relevant Contract and evidence bytes for each fixture.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=complexity-independent-of-ground-cardinality kind=requirement -->
For fixtures that add semantically irrelevant axes or domain values while holding the reduced function and declared certificate sets constant, obligations, certificate coverage representation, canonical DAG nodes and bytes must remain bounded independently of theoretical ground cardinality.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=certificate-package-recomputation kind=technical_obligation -->
For every omitted axis the package requires both Source-side and production-side proof by complete static dependency closure over frozen closed-world inputs, exact equivalence in the restricted decidable IR or genuine exhaustive equivalence over a finite complete domain; each proof binds a digest-identified frozen executable Oracle with the exact symbolic_noninterference side-and-method capability, while dynamic loading, reflection, unfrozen implicit dependencies, external devices, sampling and provider or author complete booleans block closure.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=certificate-not-semantic-proof-substitute kind=technical_obligation -->
Non-interference certificates prove only safe representation omission and sharing; they do not replace pointwise semantic Facts, required-method denotation or current production observations.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=no-obligation-per-irrelevant-axis-rule kind=requirement -->
The model does not mechanically add one semantic or certificate obligation for every newly introduced irrelevant axis when one exact set-valued certificate already covers the changed omission set.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=v1-stat-before-read kind=technical_obligation -->
V1 handoff and manifest resources are stat-checked against declared byte budgets before any whole-file read, YAML or JSON parse, hydration or Cartesian validation.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=v1-bounded-prefix-stream-parse kind=technical_obligation -->
Compact V1 manifest admission reads at most the declared bounded prefix to obtain schema version and generation collection counts before whole-file parsing; a missing or late capacity header fails with actionable guidance.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=v1-capacity-header-order kind=technical_obligation -->
V1 canonical manifest serialization places schema identity and generation collection count metadata before large collections so the bounded preparse is deterministic and does not scan the full document.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=v1-embedded-handoff-byte-limit kind=technical_obligation -->
The embedded V1 handoff hard limit is 8,388,608 bytes, derived as the next power of two above four times the measured 1,930,720-byte complete reference fixture.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=v1-manifest-byte-limit kind=technical_obligation -->
The compact V1 canonical manifest hard limit is 33,554,432 bytes, derived as the next power of two above four times the measured 4,411,120-byte complete reference fixture.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=v1-ground-cell-limit kind=technical_obligation -->
The V1 explicit expected Fact Cell hard limit is 16,384, derived as the next power of two above four times the measured 3,038-cell complete reference fixture; V2 regeneration is the required path above it.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=v1-capacity-prefix-limit kind=technical_obligation -->
The capacity-header streaming prefix is bounded to 65,536 bytes and admission rejects a manifest whose required header is not wholly available within that schema budget.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=v1-reject-before-hydration kind=requirement -->
A V1 size or expected-count violation is rejected before JSON parsing, manifest projection, condition or variation expansion and handoff hydration, with no partial acceptance.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=v1-threshold-measurement-basis kind=technical_obligation -->
Threshold evidence is the current repository complete fixture on Windows x64, Node 24.16.0 and an Intel i5-11400F: 33 condition axes, 30 variation-axis rows, 2 conditions, 6 subjects, 7 variations, 217 properties, 3,038 Fact Cells, 36 Facts and 48 proof obligations; embedded handoff 1,930,720 bytes, compact handoff 113,792 bytes and canonical manifest 4,411,120 bytes.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=v1-capacity-actionable-diagnostics kind=technical_obligation -->
Capacity rejection reports measured bytes, declared counts, limit, the largest available contributors and the exact opt-in V2 regeneration route without logging protected expected values.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=capacity-fail-closed-no-truncation kind=requirement -->
Capacity protection never truncates, samples, summarizes, lowers granularity or silently falls back to a partial V1 universe; exceeding a budget is unavailable until the producer regenerates an admitted V2 representation.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=efficiency-admission-property-only kind=requirement -->
Purpose-fulfillment efficiency non-degradation is a package mechanism-change admission and regression property evaluated before adopting or defaulting the mechanism.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=efficiency-not-terminal-safety-theorem kind=requirement -->
Purpose-fulfillment efficiency non-degradation is not part of the AcceptedDeliveryTerminal safety theorem and cannot become a delivery-completion condition.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=no-second-authority kind=technical_obligation -->
Efficiency admission creates no second Source, Contract Authority, value authority or acceptance authority.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=no-second-final-gate kind=technical_obligation -->
Efficiency admission creates no second Final Gate, package completion state or alternate machine-acceptance terminal.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=no-delivery-deadline-condition kind=requirement -->
The mechanism introduces no delivery deadline, elapsed-time acceptance predicate or fixed completion-time promise.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=no-fixed-execution-flow kind=requirement -->
The mechanism introduces no fixed implementation order, per-edit mandate, scheduler, agent-allocation rule or workflow phase beyond the existing Goal-owned freedom boundary.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=performance-claim-measurement-contract kind=technical_obligation -->
Any runtime efficiency claim must name workload, metric, baseline or budget, environment, comparator and tolerance and must be produced by a project-owned current-candidate measurement; static representation shape alone is not runtime proof.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=non-ui-v2-separate-admission kind=requirement -->
Non-UI semantic-fact V2 is outside this delivery and requires a separate admission after UI V2 proves the shared engine boundary; existing semantic-fact-manifest-v1 behavior is unchanged here.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=provider-p0-excluded kind=requirement -->
Provider trust-boundary and P0 acquisition changes are outside this delivery because no repository-reproducible failure evidence currently justifies them.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=provider-reentry-evidence-required kind=requirement -->
Provider or P0 scope may re-enter only through a separately reviewed repository-reproducible failing fixture, exact ownership analysis and Authority revision or new delivery.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=shared-engine-future-boundary kind=technical_obligation -->
The symbolic predicate and canonical DAG engine is domain-neutral and package-owned, but UI schema, applicability policy and Contract projection remain UI-owned so a future non-UI admission can reuse the engine without importing UI semantics.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=rollout-1-v1-capacity kind=requirement -->
Rollout step 1 ships and verifies V1 stat and bounded-stream capacity protection before any V2 default behavior.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=rollout-2-formal-denotation kind=requirement -->
Rollout step 2 freezes the formal V1/V2 extensional denotation and equivalence predicates before production symbolic projection is accepted.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=rollout-3-engine-independent-oracle kind=requirement -->
Rollout step 3 implements the internal symbolic engine together with an independently authored test-only full finite expander and comparator Oracle.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=rollout-4-ui-v2-opt-in kind=requirement -->
Rollout step 4 admits UI V2 only as explicit opt-in after equivalence and complexity counterexamples pass.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=rollout-5-mixed-downstream kind=requirement -->
Rollout step 5 admits mixed V1 and V2 downstream Contract and evidence closure in the same Final Gate.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=rollout-6-default-after-proof kind=requirement -->
Rollout step 6 may change the UI default only in a later Authority-backed decision after equivalence, compatibility, false-negative resistance and measured efficiency admission remain passing; this delivery does not flip the default.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=rollout-7-non-ui-provider-separate kind=requirement -->
Rollout step 7 treats non-UI V2 and any Provider trust-boundary work as separate admissions with their own evidence rather than implied follow-through.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=independent-full-finite-test-expander kind=technical_obligation -->
Tests use an independently implemented full finite extensional expander and direct denotation comparator that does not call the production DAG canonicalizer or reuse its reduction logic.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=exhaustive-pointwise-equivalence-tests kind=technical_obligation -->
Bounded exhaustive and property-generated tests compare V1 and V2 at every finite subject or relation, target, reachable condition or variation, applicable property and population or quantifier point for disposition, expected semantics and proof-obligation denotation.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=adversarial-false-completion-counterexamples kind=technical_obligation -->
Executable counterexamples independently remove or alter a condition, variation, property applicability region, relation endpoint, population quantifier, required method, expected locator, comparator, certificate edge or current result and must fail at the attributable identity.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=mixed-contract-verification kind=technical_obligation -->
Tests compile and evidence one Contract containing at least one V1 target and one V2 target and reject cross-target binding, result and version confusion.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=capacity-early-rejection-verification kind=technical_obligation -->
Tests instrument read, parse, hydrate and expansion boundaries to prove oversized V1 inputs are rejected after stat or bounded prefix inspection and before full read, JSON parse, hydration or Cartesian work.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=complexity-parameter-verification kind=technical_obligation -->
Parameter tests add irrelevant axes, values and dependency proofs and assert exact denotation stability plus bounded obligation count, certificate coverage representation, canonical DAG nodes and bytes while theoretical ground cardinality grows; a deterministic package fixture measures 639 subjects, 217 properties, 53 axes and 5,245 variations, preserves all 138,663 logical subject-property points, avoids 137,385 physical N/A rows and budgets authoring, preflight, bytes and RSS.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=distribution-context-parity kind=technical_obligation -->
Implementation, strict schemas, CLI, public types, package assets, managed workspace copies, README surfaces, PROJECT_SPEC, owning Context, implementation index, affected and Trust routing and focused tests must teach and enforce the same versioned semantics, scope and rollout.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=architecture-deliberation kind=technical_obligation aspect=architecture -->
Architecture Deliberation: owners are design-resource handoff/fact policy, package-owned applicability/index and compact-revision modules, the shared symbolic-denotation compiler, dual-side non-interference validation, Long-Task UI/evidence, compact migration Authority sync, package distribution, root Git byte policy and owning Context; extension points are V1 projection, Long-Task fact_expectations/fact_results, the revision and migration helpers, and `.gitattributes`. Selected design: opt-in UI V2 uses restricted predicates, one manifest compilation session, canonical shared DAGs, indexed lookup, profile applicability plus explicit exceptions, exclusive regions, constant located values, dual-side trusted proof, separate semantic/certificate obligations, V1 preparse guards, mixed V1/V2 Final-Gate closure and LF Git text. Digest logic, fixture bodies and Authority sync are extracted behind existing callers; test declarations and the critical sentinel stay in their reviewed suite. Dependencies run from canonical resources through frozen Inspector/Oracle TCBs, handoff validation, Contract bindings and current evidence, never backward into value Authority; repository input SHA-256 is strict raw-byte identity and state stays Contract/current-run owned. Rejected: V1 mutation, DNF/full expansion, post-expansion deduplication, physical N/A or Rule-axis matrices, full-array tuple scans, arbitrary value functions, precedence, sampling/representative proof, ungrounded N/A, historical proof reuse, non-UI or Provider/P0 bundling, digest EOL normalization, dual LF/CRLF acceptance, current-input-only path lists, modularity waivers, line compression, or relocated critical test identity. A new predicate theory, dynamic dependency or non-UI consumer requires separate decidable admission without weakening identity/TCB; genuine CRLF requires a narrow Git override and Authority revision; new digest, fixture or Authority mapping changes through its bounded owner. V1 guards, V2 profiles/shared compilation/indexes, `text=auto eol=lf` and responsibility-preserving extraction pay down Cartesian/repeated-scan, EOL and three over-budget-module debt; unrelated Provider debt stays out pending evidence. Project-owned equivalence, adversarial, real-scale capacity, mixed-version, cross-platform raw-digest, distribution, exact test-identity, modularity, full-suite, Context and Final-Gate checks prove the design. Equivalent-line-ending acceptance, CI-only skip, second digest, waiver or expanded shadow Authority is forbidden. Correctness, maintainability, compatibility, performance/capacity, reliability, operability and testability are triggered; strict grammar, bounded parsing, exact Oracle capabilities, fail-closed dependencies and protected-value semantics preserve security/privacy/safety.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=no-sampling kind=forbidden_shortcut -->
Do not use representative, pairwise, random-only or risk-selected sampling to stand in for complete extensional denotation, applicability or final proof closure.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=no-granularity-reduction kind=forbidden_shortcut -->
Do not reduce Fact, atomic property, condition, variation, relation, population, quantifier, required-method or observation-boundary granularity to obtain smaller artifacts.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=no-expand-then-deduplicate kind=forbidden_shortcut -->
Do not first materialize the ground Cartesian universe and then group, hash, compress or deduplicate it; production complexity must be symbolic from input onward.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=no-historical-proof-reuse kind=forbidden_shortcut -->
Do not reuse historical tests, receipts, Progress or a prior candidate's semantic or certificate results as current Final-Gate proof.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=no-authority-gate-proliferation kind=forbidden_shortcut -->
Do not add a second Source, Contract, Authority, Final Gate, terminal theorem, registry, package completion state or acceptance workflow for symbolic or efficiency admission.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=no-arbitrary-symbolic-execution kind=forbidden_shortcut -->
Do not admit arbitrary code, opaque predicates, implementation-owned expected-value functions or ordered override precedence into V2 Rules.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=no-ungrounded-applicability-omission kind=forbidden_shortcut -->
Do not use a missing property, omitted axis, empty Census row, broad not-applicable label or provider complete flag as evidence-backed applicability closure.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=v1-v2-denotation-equivalence-ac kind=acceptance -->
Given V1 and V2 representations of the same finite design universe, the independent expander observes set-equal pointwise disposition, expected semantics and proof-obligation denotation across every subject or relation, target, reachable condition or variation, applicable property and population or quantifier point; mutating any one component fails the exact comparison.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=canonical-region-and-applicability-ac kind=acceptance -->
Given overlapping, gapped, redundant, reordered and Census-incomplete Rule inputs, valid equivalent inputs normalize to one digest while overlap, gap, unknown applicability, missing basis, unreachable claimed coverage and nonconstant expected values each fail closed.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=mixed-contract-current-evidence-ac kind=acceptance -->
Given one Contract with V1 and V2 targets, Compile preserves each representation's exact expectations and the existing Final Gate accepts only exact current V1 fact results, V2 Rule-method-region results and recomputed certificate results with no cross-target substitution.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=noninterference-complexity-ac kind=acceptance -->
Given fixtures with increasing irrelevant axes, values and dependency edges, theoretical ground cardinality grows while denotation stays equal and obligation counts, exact set-valued certificate coverage, canonical DAG nodes and bytes remain within declared ground-independent budgets; adding a relevant dependency invalidates the certificate.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=v1-capacity-guard-ac kind=acceptance -->
Given V1 resources immediately below and above each byte, header and Fact Cell threshold, below-limit fixtures preserve V1 results and above-limit fixtures fail with measured diagnostics before prohibited full read, parse, hydration or Cartesian work; no row is truncated.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=scope-and-rollout-ac kind=acceptance -->
Given legacy V1, explicit UI V2, non-UI semantic facts and Provider inputs, V1 remains default, UI V2 requires opt-in, mixed targets work, non-UI and Provider behavior remains unchanged and no later rollout step is treated as already authorized.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=package-antidegradation-and-parity-ac kind=acceptance -->
Given the final candidate on Windows and POSIX checkouts, repository-backed text inputs have one Git-enforced LF raw-byte identity independent of ambient `core.autocrlf`, and focused equivalence, canonicalization, applicability, capacity, mixed-version, evidence, complexity and guidance tests plus the complete package suite, source parity, Context validation, Harness validation and sole Final Gate all pass from one committed snapshot without forbidden shortcuts or scope escape.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=inventory-is-not-completion kind=non_completing -->
A complete Source, semantic manifest, symbolic DAG, certificate inventory, preflight, Compile, focused test, benchmark, Progress record or prose review alone does not complete delivery; only all declared checks rerun by the existing sole Final Gate on the current committed candidate can do so.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=public-schema-change-risk kind=risk_fact fact=public_api_or_schema_change outcome=symbolic-denotation-efficiency -->
This delivery adds versioned public UI handoff, Contract and evidence schema surfaces, so strict parser/schema/type parity, compatibility diagnostics, fixtures, documentation and package distribution must change together.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=false-completion-critical-path-risk kind=risk_fact fact=critical_user_path outcome=symbolic-denotation-efficiency -->
Symbolic omission is false-completion-critical because an overlap, gap, omitted dependency, narrowed applicability set or missing method can otherwise preserve aggregate green results while changing extensional meaning.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=symbolic-oracle-observability-risk kind=risk_fact fact=weak_observability outcome=symbolic-denotation-efficiency -->
The Harness can prove declared canonicalization, denotation equality, certificate recomputation and current result closure, but semantic soundness of the frozen Inspector and independent test Oracle remains a named trusted-computing boundary requiring counterexamples and honest blocking.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->
